library(stringr)
library(dplyr)

Code
sleep <- read.csv("~/Downloads/Developer/GitHub/ds112-portfolio-crohagan/pvj/sleep.csv")
Code
sleep %>%
  mutate(
    startDate = with_tz(ymd_hms(startDate), "America/Chicago"),
    endDate   = with_tz(ymd_hms(endDate), "America/Chicago"),
    dur_hrs   = as.numeric(difftime(endDate, startDate, units = "hours"))
  )

Code
nightly <- sleep %>%
  dplyr::filter(grepl("Watch", source)) %>%
  dplyr::filter(value %in% c("AsleepCore", "AsleepDeep", "AsleepREM", "AsleepUnspecified")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(
    start   = with_tz(ymd_hms(startDate), "America/Chicago"),
    end     = with_tz(ymd_hms(endDate), "America/Chicago"),
    dur_hrs = as.numeric(difftime(end, start, units = "hours")),
    night   = as_date(end)
  ) %>%
  dplyr::filter(night >= as.Date("2026-01-01")) %>%
  dplyr::group_by(night) %>%
  dplyr::summarise(
    hours_asleep = sum(dur_hrs),
    deep = sum(dur_hrs[value == "AsleepDeep"]),
    rem  = sum(dur_hrs[value == "AsleepREM"]),
    core = sum(dur_hrs[value == "AsleepCore"])
  ) %>%
  dplyr::filter(hours_asleep >= 2)

nrow(nightly)

nightly$core <- round(nightly$core, 3)

# 1. Data prep: drop incomplete months
nightly_clean <- nightly %>%
  mutate(night = as.Date(night),
         month = month(night, label = TRUE, abbr = FALSE),
         day   = day(night)) %>%
  filter(!month %in% c("May", "October"))

# 2. Summary stats (used for panel labels, subtitle, alt text) 
month_stats <- nightly_clean %>%
  group_by(month) %>%
  summarise(avg = mean(hours_asleep), n = n(), .groups = "drop") %>%
  mutate(month_lab = sprintf("%s  |  avg %.1f h  (%d nights)",
                             as.character(month), avg, n))

avg_total <- mean(nightly_clean$hours_asleep)
avg_deep  <- mean(nightly_clean$deep)
pct_7h    <- mean(nightly_clean$hours_asleep >= 7) * 100

# 3. Long format 
nightly_long <- nightly_clean %>%
  left_join(select(month_stats, month, month_lab), by = "month") %>%
  pivot_longer(c(deep, rem, core), names_to = "stage", values_to = "hours") %>%
  mutate(stage     = factor(stage, levels = c("core", "rem", "deep"),
                            labels = c("Core", "REM", "Deep")),
         month_lab = fct_reorder(month_lab, as.integer(month)))

# 4. Text 
subtitle_txt <- str_wrap(
  sprintf("Nightly sleep averaged %.1f h, including %.1f h of deep sleep. %.0f%% of nights reached the 7 h reference line (dashed). Gaps are nights with no recorded data.",
          avg_total, avg_deep, pct_7h),
  width = 110)

alt_txt <- sprintf(
  "Small-multiple stacked bar charts, one panel per month from June to September 2026. Each bar is one night of sleep, split into deep, REM and core sleep, with deep sleep at the bottom. A dashed line marks 7 hours. Average sleep was %.1f hours per night, including %.1f hours of deep sleep. Some nights are missing.",
  avg_total, avg_deep)

# 5. Plot 
p <- ggplot(nightly_long, aes(day, hours, fill = stage)) +
  geom_col(width = 0.92) +                                   # wider bars, no borders eating area
  geom_hline(yintercept = 7, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey20", linewidth = 0.5) +
  facet_wrap(~ month_lab, ncol = 1, axes = "all_x") +        # one month per row = much wider panels
  scale_x_continuous(breaks = 1:31,                          # label every day since there's room now
                     limits = c(0.4, 31.6), expand = c(0, 0)) +
  scale_y_continuous(limits = c(0, 12), breaks = seq(0, 12, 4),
                     expand = expansion(mult = c(0, 0))) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(Deep = "#1b2a6b", REM = "#E69F00", Core = "#9ccbe8"),
                    breaks = c("Deep", "REM", "Core")) +
  labs(title    = "Sleep stages by night, June to September 2026",
       subtitle = subtitle_txt,
       x        = "Day of month",
       y        = "Hours asleep",
       fill     = "Sleep stage",
       caption  = paste0("Data: Apple Health sleep tracking  |  ",
                         "Author: Catriona O'Hagan  |  Created: ", format(Sys.Date(), "%B %d, %Y")),
       alt      = alt_txt) +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 15) +                            # larger text overall
  theme(plot.background       = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        plot.title.position   = "plot",
        plot.title            = element_text(face = "bold", size = 22),
        plot.subtitle         = element_text(color = "grey25", size = 13, lineheight = 1.1),
        plot.caption          = element_text(color = "grey30", size = 10, hjust = 0),
        strip.text            = element_text(face = "bold", hjust = 0, size = 15),
        axis.text             = element_text(size = 11, color = "grey20"),
        axis.title            = element_text(size = 13),
        legend.position       = "top",
        legend.justification  = "left",
        legend.title          = element_text(face = "bold", size = 13),
        legend.text           = element_text(size = 12),
        legend.key.size       = unit(1, "lines"),
        panel.grid.major.x    = element_blank(),
        panel.grid.minor      = element_blank(),
        panel.spacing.y       = unit(1, "lines"),
        plot.margin           = margin(10, 15, 10, 10))

# 1. Helpers + full calendar (so missing nights are visible, and June stops at 30)
fmt_hm <- function(h) {
  m <- round(h * 60)
  sprintf("%d h %02d min", m %/% 60, m %% 60)
}

calendar <- tibble(night = seq(as.Date("2026-06-01"), as.Date("2026-09-30"), by = "day")) %>%
  mutate(month    = month(night, label = TRUE, abbr = FALSE),
         day      = day(night),
         day_type = if_else(wday(night, week_start = 1) >= 6, "Weekend", "Weekday"),
         day_type = factor(day_type, levels = c("Weekday", "Weekend")))

nightly_wk <- nightly_clean %>%
  mutate(day_type = if_else(wday(night, week_start = 1) >= 6, "Weekend", "Weekday"),
         day_type = factor(day_type, levels = c("Weekday", "Weekend")))

# 2. Summary stats, now including weekday vs. weekend averages
month_stats2 <- nightly_wk %>%
  group_by(month) %>%
  summarise(avg   = mean(hours_asleep),
            n     = n(),
            wkday = mean(hours_asleep[day_type == "Weekday"]),
            wkend = mean(hours_asleep[day_type == "Weekend"]),
            .groups = "drop") %>%
  mutate(month_lab = sprintf("%s  |  avg %.1f h  (%d nights)\nWeekday avg %.1f h  |  Weekend avg %.1f h",
                             as.character(month), avg, n, wkday, wkend))

avg_wkday <- mean(nightly_wk$hours_asleep[nightly_wk$day_type == "Weekday"])
avg_wkend <- mean(nightly_wk$hours_asleep[nightly_wk$day_type == "Weekend"])

# 3. Attach panel labels to the calendar and to the nightly data
calendar <- calendar %>%
  left_join(select(month_stats2, month, month_lab), by = "month") %>%
  mutate(month_lab = fct_reorder(month_lab, as.integer(month)))

nightly_long2 <- nightly_wk %>%
  select(night, deep, rem, core) %>%
  pivot_longer(c(deep, rem, core), names_to = "stage", values_to = "hours") %>%
  mutate(stage = factor(stage, levels = c("core", "rem", "deep"),
                        labels = c("Core", "REM", "Deep"))) %>%
  left_join(calendar, by = "night")

weekend_days <- filter(calendar, day_type == "Weekend")
missing_days <- anti_join(calendar, nightly_clean, by = "night")

# label for the 7 h line, shown once (June panel only)
ref_label <- calendar %>%
  filter(month == "June", day == 30) %>%
  mutate(y = 7)

# 4. Text
subtitle_txt2 <- str_wrap(
  sprintf("Nightly sleep averaged %.1f h, including %s of deep sleep (%.1f h on weekdays vs. %.1f h on weekends). %.0f%% of nights reached the 7 h line (dashed). Shaded columns are weekends. Grey dashes mark nights with no usable data (watch not worn or charging, or under 2 h recorded).",
          avg_total, fmt_hm(avg_deep), avg_wkday, avg_wkend, pct_7h),
  width = 110)

alt_txt2 <- sprintf(
  "Small-multiple stacked bar charts, one panel per month from June to September 2026. Each bar is one night of sleep split into deep, REM and core sleep. Weekends are shaded. A dashed line marks 7 hours. Average sleep was %.1f hours on weekdays and %.1f hours on weekends. Nights with no data are marked with a short grey dash.",
  avg_wkday, avg_wkend)

# 5. Plot
p2 <- ggplot(nightly_long2, aes(day, hours)) +
  # weekend shading behind everything
  geom_rect(data = weekend_days,
            aes(xmin = day - 0.5, xmax = day + 0.5, ymin = 0, ymax = 12),
            inherit.aes = FALSE, fill = "grey90") +
  geom_col(aes(fill = stage), width = 0.8) +
  # baseline dash for nights with no data, so gaps don't look blank
  geom_segment(data = missing_days,
               aes(x = day - 0.4, xend = day + 0.4, y = 0, yend = 0),
               inherit.aes = FALSE, color = "grey55", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 7, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey20", linewidth = 0.5) +
  geom_text(data = ref_label, aes(x = day, y = y, label = "7 h"),
            inherit.aes = FALSE, vjust = -0.6, hjust = 1, size = 3.8, color = "grey20") +
  facet_wrap(~ month_lab, ncol = 1, scales = "free_x") +
  scale_x_continuous(breaks = 1:31, expand = expansion(add = 0.6)) +
  scale_y_continuous(limits = c(0, 12), breaks = c(0, 4, 8, 12),
                     labels = function(x) paste0(x, " h"),
                     expand = expansion(mult = c(0, 0))) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(Deep = "#1b2a6b", REM = "#E69F00", Core = "#9ccbe8"),
                    breaks = c("Deep", "REM", "Core")) +
  labs(title    = "Sleep stages by night, June to September 2026",
       subtitle = subtitle_txt2,
       x        = "Day of month (shaded = weekend)",
       y        = "Hours asleep",
       fill     = "Sleep stage",
       caption  = paste0("Data: Apple Health sleep tracking  |  ",
                         "Author: Catriona O'Hagan  |  Created: ", format(Sys.Date(), "%B %d, %Y")),
       alt      = alt_txt2) +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 15) +
  theme(plot.background       = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        plot.title.position   = "plot",
        plot.title            = element_text(face = "bold", size = 22),
        plot.subtitle         = element_text(color = "grey25", size = 13, lineheight = 1.1),
        plot.caption          = element_text(color = "grey30", size = 10, hjust = 0),
        strip.text            = element_text(face = "bold", hjust = 0, size = 14, lineheight = 1.1),
        axis.text             = element_text(size = 11, color = "grey20"),
        axis.title            = element_text(size = 15, face = "bold"),
        legend.position       = "top",
        legend.justification  = "left",
        legend.title          = element_text(face = "bold", size = 13),
        legend.text           = element_text(size = 12),
        panel.grid.major.x    = element_blank(),
        panel.grid.minor      = element_blank(),
        panel.spacing.y       = unit(1, "lines"),
        plot.margin           = margin(10, 15, 10, 10))

Simple version: REM sleep, weekdays vs. weekends (boxplots)

Code
# 1. Prep: all months, flag weekday vs. weekend, REM in minutes
rem_wk <- nightly %>%
  mutate(night    = as.Date(night),
         month    = month(night, label = TRUE, abbr = FALSE),
         day_type = if_else(wday(night, week_start = 1) >= 6, "Weekend", "Weekday"),
         day_type = factor(day_type, levels = c("Weekday", "Weekend")),
         rem_min  = rem * 60) %>%
  filter(night <= as.Date("2026-10-31"))   # drops the 1-2 nights in November

# 2. Overall medians for the subtitle
overall <- rem_wk %>%
  group_by(day_type) %>%
  summarise(med_min = median(rem_min), .groups = "drop")

wkday_min <- overall$med_min[overall$day_type == "Weekday"]
wkend_min <- overall$med_min[overall$day_type == "Weekend"]

subtitle_rem <- sprintf("Median REM sleep was %.0f min on weekdays and %.0f min on weekends. Each dot is one night.",
                        wkday_min, wkend_min)

# 3. Plot
p3 <- ggplot(rem_wk, aes(x = month, y = rem_min, fill = day_type)) +
  geom_boxplot(width = 0.7, outlier.shape = NA, alpha = 0.85,
               position = position_dodge(width = 0.8)) +
  geom_point(position = position_jitterdodge(jitter.width = 0.15, dodge.width = 0.8),
             size = 2, alpha = 0.5, color = "grey20") +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(Weekday = "#CBBEFF", Weekend = "#BEFFCB")) +
  scale_y_continuous(labels = function(x) paste0(x, " min"),
                     limits = c(0, NA),
                     expand = expansion(mult = c(0, 0.05))) +
  labs(title    = "REM sleep on weekdays vs. weekends",
       subtitle = subtitle_rem,
       x        = NULL,
       y        = "REM sleep per night",
       fill     = NULL,
       caption  = paste0("Data: Apple Health sleep tracking  |  ",
                         "Author: Catriona O'Hagan  |  Created: ", format(Sys.Date(), "%B %d, %Y")),
       alt      = "Boxplots of nightly REM sleep in minutes by month, comparing weekdays in grey with weekends in orange. Individual nights are shown as dots.") +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 15) +
  theme(plot.background      = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        plot.title.position  = "plot",
        plot.title           = element_text(face = "bold", size = 20),
        plot.subtitle        = element_text(color = "grey25", size = 13),
        plot.caption         = element_text(color = "grey30", size = 10, hjust = 0),
        axis.title.y         = element_text(face = "bold", size = 13),
        axis.text            = element_text(size = 11, color = "grey20"),
        legend.position      = "top",
        legend.justification = "left",
        legend.text          = element_text(size = 12),
        panel.grid.major.x   = element_blank(),
        panel.grid.minor     = element_blank())

```{r}
library(stringr)
library(dplyr)
library(lubridate)
library(ggplot2)
library(tidyverse)
```

```{r}
sleep <- read.csv("~/Downloads/Developer/GitHub/ds112-portfolio-crohagan/pvj/sleep.csv")
```


```{r}
sleep %>%
  mutate(
    startDate = with_tz(ymd_hms(startDate), "America/Chicago"),
    endDate   = with_tz(ymd_hms(endDate), "America/Chicago"),
    dur_hrs   = as.numeric(difftime(endDate, startDate, units = "hours"))
  )

nightly <- sleep %>%
  dplyr::filter(grepl("Watch", source)) %>%
  dplyr::filter(value %in% c("AsleepCore", "AsleepDeep", "AsleepREM", "AsleepUnspecified")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(
    start   = with_tz(ymd_hms(startDate), "America/Chicago"),
    end     = with_tz(ymd_hms(endDate), "America/Chicago"),
    dur_hrs = as.numeric(difftime(end, start, units = "hours")),
    night   = as_date(end)
  ) %>%
  dplyr::filter(night >= as.Date("2026-01-01")) %>%
  dplyr::group_by(night) %>%
  dplyr::summarise(
    hours_asleep = sum(dur_hrs),
    deep = sum(dur_hrs[value == "AsleepDeep"]),
    rem  = sum(dur_hrs[value == "AsleepREM"]),
    core = sum(dur_hrs[value == "AsleepCore"])
  ) %>%
  dplyr::filter(hours_asleep >= 2)

nrow(nightly)
summary(nightly$hours_asleep)
```

```{r}
nightly$hours_asleep <- round(nightly$hours_asleep, 3)

nightly$deep <- round(nightly$deep, 3)

nightly$rem <- round(nightly$rem, 3)

nightly$core <- round(nightly$core, 3)

```

```{r}
# 1. Data prep: drop incomplete months
nightly_clean <- nightly %>%
  mutate(night = as.Date(night),
         month = month(night, label = TRUE, abbr = FALSE),
         day   = day(night)) %>%
  filter(!month %in% c("May", "October"))

# 2. Summary stats (used for panel labels, subtitle, alt text) 
month_stats <- nightly_clean %>%
  group_by(month) %>%
  summarise(avg = mean(hours_asleep), n = n(), .groups = "drop") %>%
  mutate(month_lab = sprintf("%s  |  avg %.1f h  (%d nights)",
                             as.character(month), avg, n))

avg_total <- mean(nightly_clean$hours_asleep)
avg_deep  <- mean(nightly_clean$deep)
pct_7h    <- mean(nightly_clean$hours_asleep >= 7) * 100

# 3. Long format 
nightly_long <- nightly_clean %>%
  left_join(select(month_stats, month, month_lab), by = "month") %>%
  pivot_longer(c(deep, rem, core), names_to = "stage", values_to = "hours") %>%
  mutate(stage     = factor(stage, levels = c("core", "rem", "deep"),
                            labels = c("Core", "REM", "Deep")),
         month_lab = fct_reorder(month_lab, as.integer(month)))

# 4. Text 
subtitle_txt <- str_wrap(
  sprintf("Nightly sleep averaged %.1f h, including %.1f h of deep sleep. %.0f%% of nights reached the 7 h reference line (dashed). Gaps are nights with no recorded data.",
          avg_total, avg_deep, pct_7h),
  width = 110)

alt_txt <- sprintf(
  "Small-multiple stacked bar charts, one panel per month from June to September 2026. Each bar is one night of sleep, split into deep, REM and core sleep, with deep sleep at the bottom. A dashed line marks 7 hours. Average sleep was %.1f hours per night, including %.1f hours of deep sleep. Some nights are missing.",
  avg_total, avg_deep)

# 5. Plot 
p <- ggplot(nightly_long, aes(day, hours, fill = stage)) +
  geom_col(width = 0.92) +                                   # wider bars, no borders eating area
  geom_hline(yintercept = 7, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey20", linewidth = 0.5) +
  facet_wrap(~ month_lab, ncol = 1, axes = "all_x") +        # one month per row = much wider panels
  scale_x_continuous(breaks = 1:31,                          # label every day since there's room now
                     limits = c(0.4, 31.6), expand = c(0, 0)) +
  scale_y_continuous(limits = c(0, 12), breaks = seq(0, 12, 4),
                     expand = expansion(mult = c(0, 0))) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(Deep = "#1b2a6b", REM = "#E69F00", Core = "#9ccbe8"),
                    breaks = c("Deep", "REM", "Core")) +
  labs(title    = "Sleep stages by night, June to September 2026",
       subtitle = subtitle_txt,
       x        = "Day of month",
       y        = "Hours asleep",
       fill     = "Sleep stage",
       caption  = paste0("Data: Apple Health sleep tracking  |  ",
                         "Author: Catriona O'Hagan  |  Created: ", format(Sys.Date(), "%B %d, %Y")),
       alt      = alt_txt) +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 15) +                            # larger text overall
  theme(plot.background       = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        plot.title.position   = "plot",
        plot.title            = element_text(face = "bold", size = 22),
        plot.subtitle         = element_text(color = "grey25", size = 13, lineheight = 1.1),
        plot.caption          = element_text(color = "grey30", size = 10, hjust = 0),
        strip.text            = element_text(face = "bold", hjust = 0, size = 15),
        axis.text             = element_text(size = 11, color = "grey20"),
        axis.title            = element_text(size = 13),
        legend.position       = "top",
        legend.justification  = "left",
        legend.title          = element_text(face = "bold", size = 13),
        legend.text           = element_text(size = 12),
        legend.key.size       = unit(1, "lines"),
        panel.grid.major.x    = element_blank(),
        panel.grid.minor      = element_blank(),
        panel.spacing.y       = unit(1, "lines"),
        plot.margin           = margin(10, 15, 10, 10))


```

```{r}
#| fig-height: 10
p
ggsave("sleep_by_month.png", p, width = 11, height = 8, dpi = 200, bg = "white")
```

## Revised version (after feedback)

```{r}
# 1. Helpers + full calendar (so missing nights are visible, and June stops at 30)
fmt_hm <- function(h) {
  m <- round(h * 60)
  sprintf("%d h %02d min", m %/% 60, m %% 60)
}

calendar <- tibble(night = seq(as.Date("2026-06-01"), as.Date("2026-09-30"), by = "day")) %>%
  mutate(month    = month(night, label = TRUE, abbr = FALSE),
         day      = day(night),
         day_type = if_else(wday(night, week_start = 1) >= 6, "Weekend", "Weekday"),
         day_type = factor(day_type, levels = c("Weekday", "Weekend")))

nightly_wk <- nightly_clean %>%
  mutate(day_type = if_else(wday(night, week_start = 1) >= 6, "Weekend", "Weekday"),
         day_type = factor(day_type, levels = c("Weekday", "Weekend")))

# 2. Summary stats, now including weekday vs. weekend averages
month_stats2 <- nightly_wk %>%
  group_by(month) %>%
  summarise(avg   = mean(hours_asleep),
            n     = n(),
            wkday = mean(hours_asleep[day_type == "Weekday"]),
            wkend = mean(hours_asleep[day_type == "Weekend"]),
            .groups = "drop") %>%
  mutate(month_lab = sprintf("%s  |  avg %.1f h  (%d nights)\nWeekday avg %.1f h  |  Weekend avg %.1f h",
                             as.character(month), avg, n, wkday, wkend))

avg_wkday <- mean(nightly_wk$hours_asleep[nightly_wk$day_type == "Weekday"])
avg_wkend <- mean(nightly_wk$hours_asleep[nightly_wk$day_type == "Weekend"])

# 3. Attach panel labels to the calendar and to the nightly data
calendar <- calendar %>%
  left_join(select(month_stats2, month, month_lab), by = "month") %>%
  mutate(month_lab = fct_reorder(month_lab, as.integer(month)))

nightly_long2 <- nightly_wk %>%
  select(night, deep, rem, core) %>%
  pivot_longer(c(deep, rem, core), names_to = "stage", values_to = "hours") %>%
  mutate(stage = factor(stage, levels = c("core", "rem", "deep"),
                        labels = c("Core", "REM", "Deep"))) %>%
  left_join(calendar, by = "night")

weekend_days <- filter(calendar, day_type == "Weekend")

# label for the 7 h line, shown once (June panel only)

  filter(month == "June", day == 30) %>%
  mutate(y = 7)

# 4. Text

  sprintf("Nightly sleep averaged %.1f h, including %s of deep sleep (%.1f h on weekdays vs. %.1f h on weekends). %.0f%% of nights reached the 7 h line (dashed). Shaded columns are weekends. Grey dashes mark nights with no usable data (watch not worn or charging, or under 2 h recorded).",
          avg_total, fmt_hm(avg_deep), avg_wkday, avg_wkend, pct_7h),
  width = 110)

alt_txt2 <- sprintf(
  "Small-multiple stacked bar charts, one panel per month from June to September 2026. Each bar is one night of sleep split into deep, REM and core sleep. Weekends are shaded. A dashed line marks 7 hours. Average sleep was %.1f hours on weekdays and %.1f hours on weekends. Nights with no data are marked with a short grey dash.",
  avg_wkday, avg_wkend)

# 5. Plot
p2 <- ggplot(nightly_long2, aes(day, hours)) +
  # weekend shading behind everything
  geom_rect(data = weekend_days,
            aes(xmin = day - 0.5, xmax = day + 0.5, ymin = 0, ymax = 12),
            inherit.aes = FALSE, fill = "grey90") +
  geom_col(aes(fill = stage), width = 0.8) +
  # baseline dash for nights with no data, so gaps don't look blank
  geom_segment(data = missing_days,
               aes(x = day - 0.4, xend = day + 0.4, y = 0, yend = 0),
               inherit.aes = FALSE, color = "grey55", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 7, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey20", linewidth = 0.5) +
  geom_text(data = ref_label, aes(x = day, y = y, label = "7 h"),
            inherit.aes = FALSE, vjust = -0.6, hjust = 1, size = 3.8, color = "grey20") +
  facet_wrap(~ month_lab, ncol = 1, scales = "free_x") +

                     expand = expansion(mult = c(0, 0))) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(Deep = "#1b2a6b", REM = "#E69F00", Core = "#9ccbe8"),
                    breaks = c("Deep", "REM", "Core")) +
  labs(title    = "Sleep stages by night, June to September 2026",
       subtitle = subtitle_txt2,
       x        = "Day of month (shaded = weekend)",
       y        = "Hours asleep",
       fill     = "Sleep stage",
       caption  = paste0("Data: Apple Health sleep tracking  |  ",

       alt      = alt_txt2) +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 15) +
  theme(plot.background       = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        plot.title.position   = "plot",
        plot.title            = element_text(face = "bold", size = 22),
        plot.subtitle         = element_text(color = "grey25", size = 13, lineheight = 1.1),
        plot.caption          = element_text(color = "grey30", size = 10, hjust = 0),
        strip.text            = element_text(face = "bold", hjust = 0, size = 14, lineheight = 1.1),
        axis.text             = element_text(size = 11, color = "grey20"),
        axis.title            = element_text(size = 15, face = "bold"),
        legend.position       = "top",
```

## Simple version: REM sleep, weekdays vs. weekends (boxplots)

```{r}
# 1. Prep: all months, flag weekday vs. weekend, REM in minutes
rem_wk <- nightly %>%
  mutate(night    = as.Date(night),
         month    = month(night, label = TRUE, abbr = FALSE),
         day_type = if_else(wday(night, week_start = 1) >= 6, "Weekend", "Weekday"),
         day_type = factor(day_type, levels = c("Weekday", "Weekend")),
         rem_min  = rem * 60) %>%
  filter(night <= as.Date("2026-10-31"))   # drops the 1-2 nights in November

# 2. Overall medians for the subtitle
overall <- rem_wk %>%
  group_by(day_type) %>%
  summarise(med_min = median(rem_min), .groups = "drop")

wkday_min <- overall$med_min[overall$day_type == "Weekday"]
wkend_min <- overall$med_min[overall$day_type == "Weekend"]

subtitle_rem <- sprintf("Median REM sleep was %.0f min on weekdays and %.0f min on weekends. Each dot is one night.",
                        wkday_min, wkend_min)

# 3. Plot
p3 <- ggplot(rem_wk, aes(x = month, y = rem_min, fill = day_type)) +
  geom_boxplot(width = 0.7, outlier.shape = NA, alpha = 0.85,
               position = position_dodge(width = 0.8)) +
  geom_point(position = position_jitterdodge(jitter.width = 0.15, dodge.width = 0.8),
             size = 2, alpha = 0.5, color = "grey20") +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(Weekday = "#CBBEFF", Weekend = "#BEFFCB")) +
  scale_y_continuous(labels = function(x) paste0(x, " min"),
                     limits = c(0, NA),
                     expand = expansion(mult = c(0, 0.05))) +
  labs(title    = "REM sleep on weekdays vs. weekends",
       subtitle = subtitle_rem,
       x        = NULL,
       y        = "REM sleep per night",
       fill     = NULL,
       caption  = paste0("Data: Apple Health sleep tracking  |  ",
                         "Author: Catriona O'Hagan  |  Created: ", format(Sys.Date(), "%B %d, %Y")),
       alt      = "Boxplots of nightly REM sleep in minutes by month, comparing weekdays in grey with weekends in orange. Individual nights are shown as dots.") +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 15) +
  theme(plot.background      = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        plot.title.position  = "plot",
        plot.title           = element_text(face = "bold", size = 20),

        legend.justification = "left",
        legend.text          = element_text(size = 12),
        panel.grid.major.x   = element_blank(),
        panel.grid.minor     = element_blank())
```
